# Four-base P41 test baselines

## R14/B26 static-NCM partial-horizon interaction at `ebc00a015` (2026-09-30)

**Discrepancy first.** The regression is a B26/SIM-03 horizon-completeness defect found during R14 integration, not an original E02 Appendix-A failure. The exact real-NCM selector passed at the four historical refs. The complete `test_joint_simulation_horizon.py` outcomes were 24/24 at Execution, 23/24 at E02 head (one unrelated failure), 24/24 at Main, and 26/26 at prior integration `2a6501ecd`; separately, the exact `test_sim_03.py` path was Git-MISSING at Execution and Main, and passed 14/14 at E02 head and 21/21 at integration `cd9e935e5`. The selector then failed on clean pre-repair integration `bedc23d74` at `interaction_terms[0]`. The three focused selectors and an initial combined 49-case run passed in the canonical worktree while unrelated provisional R13 snapshot source/test edits were present; the N5 source and both B26 test blobs matched `ebc00a015`. The broker then reran both complete files on the clean `ebc00a015` tree: `test_joint_simulation_horizon.py` passed 28/28 and `test_sim_03.py` passed 21/21. Those clean JUnits below are the current integration-head cells; the earlier dirty-tree runs and probe receipts are retained with their scope stated. The current two-file denominator is 8 cells: `test_joint_simulation_horizon.py` has 4/4 paths present with 0 MISSING/UNRUN (one newly run clean ebc cell), while `test_sim_03.py` has 2/4 present, 2 Git-MISSING, 0 UNRUN (one newly run clean ebc cell). Inventory: `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r14-exact-ebc-p41-20260930/R14_EBC_CELLS.json@sha256:80730562dc051a9dc9d9bc61f6396c898f6ef7299e0875822d799671c82b4c33`. The broker parsed per-test identities and found zero common `passed -> failed` outcomes across the historical-to-ebc comparisons; the lone E02-head joint-file failure is `failed -> passed`, and SIM-03 has 14 shared passes plus seven added ebc passes. Exact paths, outcomes, and Git-MISSING cells are in `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r14-p41-inventory-20260930/R14_P41_INVENTORY.md@sha256:43f36ac1659f52b775f0bb932929878dc466e29d8acba48d2f2eb3f3163b3be1`. This does not complete the wider 344-file / 1,376-cell touched-test matrix. The clean ebc runs used one process group and left 11,411,868 KiB free, above the 8-GiB floor; exact-cell RSS and swap growth were not measured. The manual launch locale differed from the historical harness (`LANG=en_US.UTF-8`, `LC_ALL` unset versus C/C.UTF-8), so exact environment equality is not established.

The runtime now distinguishes a valid observed scope from a scope complete over the entire requested horizon. It retains and calculates the NCM interaction (`by_step={0: 5.0}`) at the common observed step 0, while preserving `horizon_incomplete:*`, no complete checked orders for the partial horizon, and `interaction_evidence_incomplete` in the candidate value packet's authority blockers. The static multi-step zero-effect control stays `unsupported`; the complete one-step control remains additive. No missing steps are synthesized and no authority is gained.

**Behavioral evidence.** Commit `ebc00a015dc6f2fb8ad0ae948403274405bfe7e9` pins N5 source `joint_simulation_horizon.py@git-blob:b6f1341396d492fbce04b1ac39ac4cae81f65de6`, real-NCM test `test_joint_simulation_horizon.py@git-blob:9504676c5501e8a8e8909fac04c5b567c918b5ba`, and static-control tests `test_sim_03.py@git-blob:37e14aaa0b02e2e88089b2c45f2ecbaba48dadf3`.

## R14/B26 N8 candidate-only incomplete-interaction limitation at `226884f53` (2026-09-30)

**Discrepancy first.** At `ebc00a015`, N8 used one limitation set for both candidate retention and EvalSafety admission, so it rejected a content-verified N5 result with a real step-0 interaction and an explicit incomplete 0–3 horizon. Commit `226884f533e82d9e69c3d660b4a410c255c2c30a` adds a distinct N8 candidate set and leaves the EvalSafety authority set unchanged. This is candidate-band evidence only: N8 may emit `value_conditional` at `simulate_only` / low grade while retaining `interaction_evidence_incomplete`; it creates no value or method receipt, enters no EvalSafety input, and claims no N9 promotion. The patch is `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r14-n8-candidate-limitation-20260930/R14_N8_CANDIDATE_LIMITATION.patch@sha256:2ccaebc974cd6eb51fe9dc2d70f4c63f0a439db172a7f8db5b1dc2c38f903922`; design details are `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r14-n8-candidate-limitation-20260930/R14_N8_CANDIDATE_LIMITATION_DESIGN.md@sha256:b7b48d3fcfaa15c57d12257ba45ec06a0aba4fd955a5301cafa17eabd7ef1f02`; independent review is **GO, human review required** at `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/state/plugins/codex-security/scans/e02-r14-n8-candidate-limitation-20260930/artifacts-3218a7fbad9eee9466d9dc13ed474862e722da7e1ca7bf0444a205dd33474fe2/artifacts/R14_N8_CANDIDATE_LIMITATION_REVIEW.md@sha256:7a03830a8351ffc28f8f5058e00e8d46363ecba7c88991d9260bc9cccd8fba36`.

The two focused selectors were run on the patch-applied source/test tree before commit `226884f`; that commit's source/test blobs remain unchanged at current HEAD `74511e7465dd6e7795a5ade1acf202aca6abf42f`. The whole-file JUnit itself does not carry a source digest. No four-base replay was run for this test file, so the three whole-file failures below have **unresolved provenance**: do not call them inherited or assign them to a P41 base/head transition.

The three whole-file failures are `test_post_n9_packet_binds_exact_subject_and_gate_receipt` (`confidence_ledger_refused`) and `test_owner_program_graph_n5_consumes_distinct_wmr_states_and_n8_keeps_limits` plus `test_owner_program_graph_n5_state_handoff_removal_probe_keeps_markers_red` (tenant `get_paths` ownership errors). **N9 and external-surface residual.** The partial test reports `not_promoted`, but its contract-testing controller has no `PromotionRuntime` and returns before canonical `CanonicalN9PromotionPort`. The test is therefore not a direct N9-refusal witness. The review found source-level guards supporting the refusal: a conditional low-grade value cannot carry owner receipts or become `value_ready`, and the EvalSafety allowlist remains narrower. The next N9 witness must enter the real canonical port and retain a complete one-step control. B26 also remains partial because no typed external/per-job diagnostic consumer exposes the N5 observed-step, requested-horizon, checked-order, and residual details. No status or aggregate count changes: 282 rows, 7 closed / 262 partial / 12 held / 1 open.

nonempty refs retained. This is test construction, **not** a passed behavior

`/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r1-controlled-owner-bridge-audit-20260930/R1_CONTROLLED_OWNER_BRIDGE_PATCH_DELTA_REVIEW.md@sha256:6af894b1b2d5ada6457ada8e1c4879f699f90debbe5e5841195e34d8c9bae57a`.

**Property and falsifier.** For write-through exact-view operations (`has`, `get_bytes`, `get_manifest`, `verify`), the composite resolves the exact view through its durable manifest owner before local cache admission. Removing owner admission while retaining markers turns the tenant-B test red: cached bytes are returned and the refusal assertion fails (1/1 expected failure), `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r9-cache-code-20260929/TENANT_GUARD_ALL_READS_REMOVAL2.xml@sha256:d0a7d5eee21d80f401f31ee119ac2f591509a59c8240e6e76d938505474e900f`; the restored preserving control is `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r9-cache-code-20260929/TENANT_GUARD_ALL_READS_GREEN.xml@sha256:69bc2b459e918e811a51683fe94ec7e27ce3ea7a3a27c5d44cd1a0ae3fca6a97`. Integrated blobs: `policy-engine/src/polisyos/core/artifacts/backends/caching_store.py@git-blob:2144bef821ae1c4a35d3e3b7ffaec1191269213a`; `policy-engine/tests/unit/core/artifacts/backends/test_caching_store.py@git-blob:404f80fb7c7f1829bf167fce41e03f12585d284e`.

**P37/P38 and residual.** The caller tenant is supplied by request context; the ambient filesystem owner recomputes ownership on exact-manifest resolution. Cloud IAM is not established. A local cache's view-presence check does not itself test caller authorization; the bounded proof depends on a durable owner that enforces tenant scope. This evidence does not establish an HTTP-served action, deployed S3/GCS tenant policy, cloud IAM, or selected detached-signature custody. The fake-client S3/GCS suite covers manifest views and integrity, not signing or deployed IAM. Detailed missing cloud contract, principal and owner-appointment premises remain in `policy-engine/docs/plans/active/agent-packages/PolicyOS_E02R2/OPEN_PREMISES.md@git-blob:c46212afdcf9374ea7ae372b0209d5ebab7db800`; no such premise is closed by these test results. Keep R9 partial; this receipt changes no finding status or ledger count.

## Discrepancies and scope

The earlier initial and five controlled-timeout receipts did not record effective source import origins. The shared venv's editable `.pth` pointed at the execution-base checkout outside pytest; repository `conftest.py` may then prepend each checkout's source during pytest startup. Those receipts do not prove that the wrong code was imported, but their source identity was not established. This matrix requires checkout-local `PYTHONPATH` and verifies `polisyos` and `tools` origins before execution. It reuses 244 completed checkpoint rows only after pinned checkout/origin, per-row `PYTHONPATH`, env/command, source-blob, and JUnit SHA checks; the earlier manifest without explicit source-import policy additionally requires its exact legacy-harness digest and matching row evidence. It does not reuse initial or timeout-rerun result files. The three retained timeout-rerun receipts are: `raw/p41-timeout-rerun-20260924T112317Z-14479/results.json` SHA-256 `329fd52e8edd35305239b9df8f3dcb90490d607c74fa3ab68db8282ecb0109fe`; `raw/p41-timeout-rerun-20260924T120847Z-43051/results.json` SHA-256 `2ca915315561380663219051d31060dbd7feec1c88b470cdcce86bd154e2a370`; `raw/p41-timeout-rerun-20260924T122853Z-47867/results.json` SHA-256 `96a794d6102c2e46ec72d2ae763c2bd340f7cbb95cee87846765ba3d9e4a540a`.

The original 7-group initial wave had no resource samples and remains provisional (`raw/p41-20260924T084614Z-77175/results.json` SHA-256 `7df2cf49eba69875882f5dd91916ce3c3fce582a75e168adf9e52e7da0b260e5`). Its 300 moved JUnit/log artifacts are byte-mapped in `raw/p41-initial-artifact-path-remap.json` SHA-256 `b03159665e4d8f6e6ab88d2863a973cdb4a098c29cb50ab099727c748770f488`; the six no-byte timeout artifacts are represented explicitly. The source-import discrepancy is resolved only for this controlled replay.

This invocation admits at most 4 pytest process groups (harness maximum 5; CLI default 5). A light profile must finish within 60 seconds, use 65536–1310720 KiB RSS and at most 100.0% CPU, with zero swap growth. Each launch is staggered by 0.5 seconds and rechecked against current process-group RSS/CPU, memory free percent, swap and disk. Reserve admission projects 1.25x RSS and the remaining peak-growth budget of every active profiled group, plus the candidate against free memory above the 30% hard reserve. Projected admission CPU is capped at 500.0%; the sampled running hard limit is 600.0% to tolerate short accounting jitter while remaining below seven CPU cores. Profiles are scoped to the pinned runtime revision and test blob. Each revision/path cell in this matrix is unique; completed exact cells are reused rather than dispatched again. This pre-repair scope has no safe profiled overlap. Unprofiled, native, measured-heavy and resource-exclusive groups run alone and are scheduled after bounded ordinary cells. Each child is sampled every five seconds. A red or unavailable guard pauses dispatch and checkpoints unstarted cells as UNRUN. SIGINT/SIGTERM sets a cooperative stop event; active workers terminate only their own PGID, and the scheduler drains and checkpoints active and unstarted cells as UNRUN. Live descendants left after the leader exits are targeted for termination; survivors of SIGKILL stop further admission.

Resource measurements: FAIL; hard resource guard in 52 present cells (aggregate_cpu_hard_limit_exceeded); resource coverage incomplete for 51/338 present non-skipped cells; 287 cells have complete measurements. 287 cells have complete resource samples.

The requested denominator is **100 test paths** (`policy-engine/tests/**/*.py`, selected by exact Appendix A/B, R14, hardening, and planned touched-file paths). The complete tracked-test census counted 10548 Python paths across the four pinned Git trees; requested-file availability is recorded per revision below.

The four-base matrix has 400 cells: 342 present and executed or explicitly UNRUN, 58 verified missing. Missing means the exact path is absent from that revision's tracked Git tree; it is not a pass. The whole-file SHA is retained as context; per-case attribution uses the exact test AST and statically resolved test-input closure.

Shared runner: Python 3.14.0, pytest 9.0.2; CPU JAX. Main-revision timing pilots use three times a previously controlled duration for known long files (or an explicit 1800s pilot ceiling); other bases use three times the larger of the measured main and previously controlled whole-file durations, with a 120s floor when the pilot has cases. If the pilot has no cases, sibling alarms include its alarm and use at least 1800s and the previous controlled ceiling, if any; the pilot's actual status is recorded separately. Runtime revisions cannot share a light profile merely because test bytes match; each unprofiled sibling runs alone.

Production data input: `/Users/deniskopylov/polisyos/policy-engine/production_data/manifest.json` SHA-256 `9e0e0aa0acd3c91f0120a80a2570be358ff16a63218abcd998f4d6f0212b6105`. The path resolved identically from all four checkouts; preflight also verifies that the production_data root and manifest have no write bits (the per-checkout mode and read-only verdict are in `results.json`). Environment values were allowlisted; `.env` loading was disabled and no environment values are recorded.

The harness predicate is: a selected test case's status at a fixed Git revision and test-source blob under the shared local environment. It reads Git HEAD/tree/blob identities, the production-data manifest hash, pytest JUnit case status, and process exit status. Exit status checks runner integrity; JUnit supplies the per-case result. `UNRUN` means the harness could not inspect/execute a cell. Native-resource isolation is detected from direct imports/use in each test module; transitive or dynamic native use is `unresolved_by_construction`.

P38 divergence: the property is the actual test-case outcome; the implementation reads JUnit testcase status and uses process exit only as a runner-integrity signal. A divergent case is a test that passed but pytest exits nonzero after a session/plugin error, or a missing/incomplete JUnit artifact; those cells are marked `FAIL` or `UNRUN`, never green by exit code alone. P41 attribution compares the exact testcase AST and test-input closure: method/decorators, class context, imports, marks, fixtures/hooks, local helpers/constants, and applicable conftest files. The full test-file SHA is recorded but is not an attribution gate. Dynamic or unresolved test-side dependencies stay `not_established`; identical method AST with changed/unmeasured fixture scope is reported separately.

R6 named-case note: `policy-engine/tests/unit/runtime/quality/test_generation_cycle.py` / `tests.unit.runtime.quality.test_generation_cycle::test_joint_port_rejects_candidate_unbound_resolution_from_another_context` has identical method ASTs across all four bases but differing test-context signatures, and at least one input closure is unresolved (see `r6_discriminating_case_inputs` in `results.json`). Any observed pass-to-fail remains recorded as `PASS_TO_FAIL`; the closure difference withholds owner attribution, not the R6 repair requirement.

## Exact Appendix A/B named cases

The tracked harness embeds 74 exact required identities (37 A, 37 B). The earlier identity-source receipt is optional provenance only (status: `verified`); it is never used for outcomes. A required case is `UNRUN` if its exact JUnit key is missing or either relevant suite cell is missing, incomplete, or `UNRUN`.

Historical initial-receipt outcomes: 74/74; `PASS_TO_FAIL`=26, `SAME`=1, `UNRUN`=47. The rows below preserve that initial snapshot and are not the latest per-case classification. Later paired receipts resolve 27 of the 47 initial `UNRUN` identities: 16 become `PASS_TO_FAIL` and 11 become `SAME`; the v10 closeout for the same 74 identities is 42 / 12 / 20. The remaining 20 stay `UNRUN` because SIGINT cancelled the E02-head whole-file run before terminal JUnit, so no result is inferred from partial observations. **Bounded witness.** The exact test `test_parallel_tier_checkpoint_survives_stop_without_reapplying_any_peer` runs the existing executor and built-in `CASCheckpointHook`, then starts a new interpreter with Python multiprocessing `spawn`. The child receives the CAS root, run ID, serialized bundle ref and queue; it creates a new `FileSystemCAS`, resolves `seed=29,left=1,right=2` with exactly `completed_nodes=[left,right]`, and resumes to `seed=29,left=1,right=2,final=True`. Child counters show no peer rerun and one final-node execution; the parent verifies a distinct PID. This tests a persisted read across a process boundary after a simulated stop. It does not kill the writer during publication.

The positive spawned selector passed 1/1: `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-b73-fresh-store-20260929/spawn-positive.xml@sha256:d7576936f2f0c70e68f58c044be0656b635397168e3077116ce3cee8035b7f85`. ## P41 current-integration snapshot reconciliation (2026-09-26 addendum)

### Discrepancy first

The historical 84-cell table above uses Phase 0 `73c6567` as its fourth base, so its `62 complete / 20 missing / 2 UNRUN` total applies to that historical four-base selection. It is not the current-integration result. The requested current source/test snapshot is `40f498593d515006634d8989023c1ac44e24a193`; current branch HEAD is `5034ed5402915639269c927d12bbc5be5a2a587f`. The `34198535530cae11b9016f4bbd9161c7b7dda150..40f498593d515006634d8989023c1ac44e24a193` and `40f498593d515006634d8989023c1ac44e24a193..5034ed5402915639269c927d12bbc5be5a2a587f` diffs contain package-document changes only and no `policy-engine/tests` or `policy-engine/src` paths. Thus the strict test/source snapshot and whole-file P41 cell status recorded for 40f also describe HEAD 5034; this is blob/path equivalence, not a new test run.

### Strict Appendix A/B whole-file cell counts

The denominator remains 21 unique files × 4 bases = 84 cells. Here `complete` means a whole-file JUnit receipt is recorded; the count does not state whether its cases passed or failed. `missing` means the file is absent from that base's Git tree. `UNRUN` means the file is present but its whole-file P41 JUnit cell is not recorded.

| **Total** | **42** | **20** | **22** |

The present-current whole-file replay is `UNRUN` for all 21 strict files. Selected-case and class-specific receipts elsewhere in this file do not fill these whole-file cells. In particular, no current-head whole-file P41 outcome is asserted here, and `BASELINES_DONE` remains unclaimed.

The worker exception was on the E02-head `test_normative_evidence_intake.py` cell (blob shared at all refs). Its traceback enters `_run_job`'s timeout branch with a planned timeout of **152 s**, then `os.killpg(process.pid, SIGTERM)` raises `PermissionError: [Errno 1] Operation not permitted`. The row is `UNRUN` with no JUnit and loses `elapsed_seconds`, `returncode`, and `timed_out` (`None`); its recorded `timeout_basis` explicitly says whether the alarm fired is not established. This is a harness/process-cleanup failure, not a pytest assertion red. Eight later present cells carry the scheduler-paused reason from this worker failure.

**Measured property slices.** `test_lock_registry_is_bounded_without_evicting_active_waiters` requests locks for 3,000 deterministic ArtifactIDs and checks the actual returned identity set is contained in the fixed pool captured from that FileSystemCAS instance. A same-ID waiter makes a nonblocking probe while the holder owns the lock, remains excluded during the ID sweep, and enters only after the holder releases. `test_colliding_lock_stripe_keeps_distinct_cas_bytes_and_manifests` finds distinct payload-derived IDs on one stripe and reads both payloads and typed manifests back. `test_concurrent_writers_publish_only_the_persisted_first_writer_profile` checks two successful same-ID `put_bytes` calls and returned views against persisted profiles. The second-thread event is set immediately before its `put_bytes` call, so the test does not establish deterministic overlap at the lock boundary. The whole file is 17/17: `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-b153-implementation-20260929/full-v4-current.xml@sha256:7b9aa6189b7dff0ca7e6fd16c0e8fd036236256b2ae14fed27403c80443bfaf0`. Alongside that profile control, the tests cover direct same-key waiter exclusion before release and colliding-ID byte/manifest readback; they do not cover waiter progress after the first writer raises.

The marker-retaining per-ID-lock mutant turns the 3,000-ID cardinality assertion red (`/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-b153-implementation-20260929/unbounded-v4-red.xml@sha256:788a88acf3ea20c2aa44dab74b486f45f79a3e3107d59a35923d104c2cb2abd0`). A fresh-lock-per-lookup mutant turns the held same-ID waiter assertion red (`/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-b153-implementation-20260929/fresh-v4-red.xml@sha256:7176060bb78c4042a716cf2561e6533cc386c76f368c588cdf676f4b1ef66224`). `test_existing_corrupt_blob_is_not_confirmed_by_successful_retry` covers a corrupt-blob refusal, but does not contend a same-ID waiter or read back a distinct scoped view afterward. Thus the exception acceptance is not inferred from that test.

**Next step and boundary.** Proposed selector (not present or run): `tests/unit/remediation/test_cas_01.py::test_exception_releases_same_id_lock_for_scoped_view_waiter`. Hold the first same-ID scoped-view writer in the real CAS write path behind a controlled hook; synchronize on the distinct-view writer's actual acquire attempt for the same lock/stripe (a thread-start event before put_bytes is insufficient); then make the holder raise. Require the declared error, waiter completion, and exact persisted byte/manifest view readback. Include a same-profile green control. Preserve the existing fixed-pool/waiter/collision tests. This is a focused test of an acceptance gap, not a request for a lock-service redesign. Cross-process/distributed locking, long-lived production cardinality, and RSS economics remain outside the local card property. The source-free removal script is `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-b153-implementation-20260929/probe_b153_lock_removal.py@sha256:a0287fed6b65234c046c9b3626674ab3ae4388d323a41beb3d75f9f116ca31f2`; independent review of the existing bounded witness is `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-b153-implementation-20260929/B153_LOCK_WITNESS_V4_INDEPENDENT_REVIEW.md@sha256:5cac5b1e4b990bdac71bc4b08dbea5e90f2852a8c9a921879bf87fafe014f561`.

**Status effect.** B153 remains partial and ledger counts stay **7 closed / 262 partial / 12 held / 1 open**. Appendix C's “in-process locks only” residual is refined: distributed locking is not required by the card, while the missing same-process exception/waiter/scoped-view assertion is still a genuine residual. **P37/P38 boundary.** P37: the class module/name check selects structural compatibility, while local Pydantic validation recomputes the schema. It rejected a schema-valid value retained across module reload, and the typed registry boundary exposed the same defect one step later. The normalizer fixes those measured boundaries. The production use of a typed-stack SearchController remains not_established; the patch makes no served-flow or authority claim. **P37/P38 limit and independent producer gap.** The DTO recomputes equality/uniqueness of the supplied sets. In the ordinary `PolicyArtifactBuilder._build_frontier_report` path, however, `source_feasible_candidate_hashes` is copied from `projection.eligible_candidate_hashes` and `eligibility_unknown_candidate_hashes` remains empty. The ordinary producer has no independent source-feasible or unknown-eligibility input; validation of those caller-provided fields cannot establish the source denominator. Under P37, equality of supplied sets is `recomputed`, while upstream candidate completeness and absence of unknown eligibility are `not_established`. Under P38, the divergent case is a source-feasible candidate omitted before projection: it is absent from both self-derived sets, so equality remains green. Do not describe this path as independently reconciled to an observed source denominator.

The smallest closure is an independent owner-produced source-feasible identity set plus an explicit unknown-eligibility set, bridged into the ordinary report builder, with a semantic negative test for an eligible candidate omitted before projection and a separate explicit-unknown control. Current capability labels: `producer_missing`, `bridge_missing`, `semantic_test_missing`, and `surface_missing`. B111 remains `partial`. The direct champion probe does not establish production non-finite emission. Next steps also include a typed finite-hypervolume overflow outcome, production producer→consumer/persistence evidence, and the complete 21-file P41 matrix.

## R11 blocked-terminal consumer census at source pin `1e4618ae3` (2026-09-30; source-only)

**Discrepancies first.** This is a complete tracked-source census, not a behavioral run. The memo pins reads to immutable source commit `1e4618ae35713b665f1521c3e3f111d395adbb49`; its observed moving branch ref was `7e9c43f1185170a130e324b0fef4ef28e78749d2`. The memo does not reconcile intervening/current source changes after that pin. It ran no tests; the marker-retaining removal probe below is specified, not executed. It therefore does not fill any current four-base P41 cell or close R11.

The census walked 2,696 tracked Python files under `policy-engine/src`, 2,744 under `policy-engine/tests`, and 1,231 tracked TS/TSX/JS/JSX files under `policy-engine/apps` and `policy-engine/packages`. At that pinned tree, `LoopVOIDecision` occurs only in the canonical generation-cycle owner and generated history schema; direct production `.voi_decision` reads are confined to that owner. The named frontend identifier family has zero matches in all 1,231 files. Downstream production consumers receive the owner run or its derived terminal rather than the N6 decision object.

**Owner and consumer path.** The canonical owner reconciles a blocked VOI action before N7 and after the cycle loop, projects it to a blocked run, and sets N9 to `not_promoted` before promotion. The same pre-N9 run-status branch stops other blocked causes (cycle cap, fake-cycle/progress guard, and no-new-grammar guard); it has no cause allow-list. Recursive routing derives a blocked terminal from the owner run, and recursive validation checks child/parent terminal projections against the persisted source. Persisted evaluation-safety and public-export consumers call the same N9 eligibility owner and refuse a blocked leaf before receipt parsing. The normative S8 projection retains candidate fronts and returns a blocked request without invoking its ranking owner.

Two bounded downstream gaps remain. The cycle-board projection displays the owner terminal for known inputs, but production recursive-run enumeration is recorded as `absent/unallocated` (`artifact_missing`, `bridge_missing`, `exhaustive=false`); complete N6 action-level board coverage is therefore `not_established`. Acquisition re-entry preserves the full blocked source run in its content-bound receipt, while the movement summary carries only terminal kind and receipt reference; action-level meaning is not denormalized onto that row. This is a summary-surface limit, not an N9 bypass found by the census.

**P37/P38.** The measured property is that no current-schema N6 run containing a blocked action can continue into N9. The gate returns no eligible N9 source for a blocked run; completed runs still undergo owner validation that recomputes blocked-action membership/finality and reconciles the run status, reason, projections, and N9 fields. A marker-retaining divergent case—change only `terminal_status` to `completed` while retaining the blocked action, reason, `block_candidate`, `blocked_no_retry`, and `not_promoted` marker—is expected to be rejected by semantic validation and by persisted evaluation-safety before receipt parsing. The memo did not execute this removal probe.

The cap-cause basis is weaker: persisted `GenerationCycleRun` has no `max_cycles` field, and the validator accepts the cap reason plus final `next_action="advance"` shape without proving the configured cap equals the cycle count. P37 therefore labels cap attainment `not_established`; under P38, a shorter otherwise valid run forged with the cap reason could satisfy that cause branch. This limits confidence in the recorded *reason* for blocking, while the blocked run remains ineligible for N9.

The memo read current test sources for blocked-action/N7/N9, persisted-receipt, public-export, and S8 controls; these were not run by this census. Its report of a prior 7/7 focused R11 run and a source-level N9-guard removal mutation that made two negatives fail is historical evidence from an earlier receipt, not a fresh result at this source pin. R11 remains partial. The complete memo, including source line map and all test identities, is `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/e02-r11-blocked-terminal-consumer-matrix-20260930.md@sha256:1646b89054cfd8e0821326ddb0d6ebf3446dd6739afe309bc8942b9f4eeef9b7`.

## R10 guarded signed-store capability and N9 custody witness at `fe246c65b` (2026-09-30)

**Discrepancies first.** The pre-R10 whole-file bridge JUnit at exact parent/base `0ea8137ae01605a6749bfc4a8cc7b42f8db69ec9` contains 13 cases: 2 passed, 2 failed, and 9 errored. The post-R10 whole-file JUnit contains 14 cases: 3 passed, 2 failed, and 9 errored. All 13 common test identities retain the same outcome; the only added case, `test_n9_repository_signature_capability_preserves_guarded_tenant_cell_custody`, passes. The 11 red outcomes are therefore demonstrated at the exact pre-R10 `0ea` bridge baseline and remain present after R10. This comparison does not attribute them to R1 or R14. The complete four-base P41 replay remains **UNRUN**, so no broader owner attribution follows.

**Bounded implementation and behavior.** Commit `fe246c65bfa3f06fe1987ae78ae0147e3e1ebe67` adds the separate optional `SignatureVerifyingArtifactStore` protocol, selects the filesystem verifier from the configured backend, and carries the same guarded store handle into the canonical N9 promotion-safety owner. `_source_attempt` parses the requested value once to `ArtifactID`, uses that identity for CAS and verifier reads, checks the verifier’s returned artifact ID, and persists typed signature-attempt outcomes in request schema v2 while retaining the historical v1 projection. The focused N9 repository custody selector passed 1/1; the complete promotion-safety unit file passed 22/22. Ruff exited 0 across all 11 changed Python files. The JUnit metadata does not embed a Git revision; code and test identities are pinned below at the R10 commit.

The guarded N9 repository witness exercises a same-tenant/cell positive and cross-tenant/cell refusal. Foreign request replay is refused before source attempts; separate calls then exercise the existing owner’s guarded source read and signature capability. This is an N9 repository/owner/capability witness, not a served `ControlWorker` tenant-custody witness. The worker path still lacks the R14 tenant-scope bridge: `_process_control_job` reads tenant-claimed payload before `_job_tenant_scope(payload)`, and the test does not install `tenant_scope` around dispatch. Promotion authority remains `not_established`.

**Caller census and remaining boundary.** A full tracked-tree walk at `fe246c65b` covered 2,696 `.py` files under `policy-engine/src/` and 445 `.py` files under `policy-engine/tools/`. It found 28 signature-verification definition/call matches: 9 definitions and 19 calls, counting the internal `_verify_signature` call in the concrete store. The census script is `/Users/deniskopylov/.codex/scratch/E02R2-R10-signed-store-candidate-20260930/R10-signature-census.py@sha256:a5f7e6f4cbbda7dcdbe8c02a0e4b3e34dc81604df3716408de7763d0e2aff9c1`. The R10 promotion owner no longer sends its raw string to the verifier. Separate string-shaped callers remain in `value_choice_provenance.py:821` and `obligation_coverage.py:532,589`; this slice does not establish their behavior across non-filesystem verifier implementations.

P37: `backend == "filesystem"` is the typed composition selector for this optional capability; the deployed backend remains `not_established`. P38: the protected property is source attribution against the exact normalized ID and bytes from the same guarded store; the implementation checks the same store identity and returned verifier ID. A worker dispatch that reads tenant-claimed payload before applying its tenant scope is a divergent case, so the repository-level negative cannot establish worker custody. The bounded filesystem path has a typed contract, producer, persisted v2 outcome, orchestration bridge, N9 consumer, and focused verification. Non-filesystem verifier producers remain `producer_missing`; the served worker tenant-custody semantic witness remains `semantic_test_missing`. No promotion-authority or external dashboard/API capability is claimed.
